   16: =head1 NAME
   17: 
   18: HTML::D3 - A simple Perl module for generating charts using D3.js.
   19: 
   20: =head1 VERSION
   21: 
   22: Version 0.30
   23: 
   24: =cut
   25: 
   26: our $VERSION = '0.30';
   27: 
   28: # A Content-Security-Policy nonce: base64 or base64url, so it cannot end the
   29: # attribute it is written into.
   30: my $NONCE_RE = qr{\A[A-Za-z0-9+/_=-]+\z};
   31: 
   32: =head1 SYNOPSIS
   33: 
   34:     use HTML::D3;
   35: 
   36:     my $chart = HTML::D3->new(
   37:         width => 1024,
   38:         height => 768,
   39:         title => 'Sample Bar Chart'
   40:     );
   41: 
   42:     my $data = [
   43:         ['Category 1', 10],
   44:         ['Category 2', 20],
   45:         ['Category 3', 30]
   46:     ];
   47: 
   48:     my $html = $chart->render_bar_chart($data);
   49:     print $html;
   50: 
   51:     $chart = HTML::D3->new(title => 'Sales Data');
   52: 
   53:     $data = [
   54:         ['Product A', 100],
   55:         ['Product B', 150],
   56:         ['Product C', 200]
   57:     ];
   58: 
   59:     $html = $chart->render_line_chart($data);
   60:     print $html;
   61: 
   62: =head1 DESCRIPTION
   63: 
   64: HTML::D3 is a Perl module that provides functionality to create simple charts using D3.js.
   65: The module generates HTML and JavaScript code to render the chart in a web browser.
   66: 
   67: Text in a chart's data and options is written into a C<E<lt>scriptE<gt>> so that it
   68: cannot end the script: C<E<lt>> is written as C<\u003c>, which JavaScript reads back
   69: as the same character.  An C<id> is written into an attribute, a selector and a
   70: script, so it must be letters, digits, C<_> and C<->, not starting with a digit or
   71: C<->.  Any other C<id> is ignored and the method's default used, so read the id a
   72: method actually used from the C<svg_id> or C<table_id> it returns.
   73: 
   74: =head1 METHODS
   75: 
   76: The C<=head3 API SPECIFICATION> subsections use L<Params::Validate::Strict>
   77: schema syntax (C<< type => 'arrayref' >> etc.) as a documentation convention.
   78: The module is also used at runtime in C<new()> to validate constructor
   79: arguments; it is therefore a required runtime dependency.  The schemas describe
   80: the parameter contract in machine-readable notation and can be plumbed into a
   81: WAF or test generator if desired.
   82: 
   83: =head2 new
   84: 
   85:     my $chart = HTML::D3->new(%args);
   86: 
   87: Creates a new HTML::D3 object.
   88: Accepts the following optional arguments:
   89: 
   90: =over 4
   91: 
   92: =item * C<width> - The width of the chart (default: 800).
   93: 
   94: =item * C<height> - The height of the chart (default: 600).
   95: 
   96: =item * C<title> - The title of the chart (default: 'Chart').
   97: It is plain text, escaped when it is written to the page: C<E<lt>> and C<&> appear
   98: as themselves, and HTML in a title is shown rather than obeyed.
   99: 
  100: =item * C<nonce> - A Content-Security-Policy nonce.  Every C<E<lt>scriptE<gt>>
  101: element the object writes, inline or not, then carries C<nonce="...">, so a page
  102: whose policy is C<script-src 'nonce-...'> runs the chart without
  103: C<'unsafe-inline'>.  It must be made of the characters a base64 or base64url
  104: nonce uses (C<A-Z a-z 0-9 + / _ - =>), so it cannot end the attribute.  Give a
  105: fresh one for each response, and never add it to markup afterwards: a nonce put
  106: on every C<E<lt>scriptE<gt>> of a finished page would bless one injected through
  107: the data as well.  Without it the elements are written as before.
  108: 
  109: =item * C<responsive> - If true, SVG elements are rendered with
  110: C<viewBox> and C<width="100%"> instead of fixed pixel dimensions, so the
  111: chart scales fluidly with its container; with no C<height> attribute, the
  112: height follows the C<viewBox>'s proportions.  (C<height="auto"> is not a
  113: valid value for the attribute: Chromium logs an error for it and ignores it.)
  114: For snippet methods the per-call C<< opts => { responsive => 1 } >>
  115: takes precedence over this object-level setting (default: 0).
  116: 
  117: =back
  118: 
  119: =cut

  241: =head2 categorical_schemes
  242: 
  243:     my $names = HTML::D3->categorical_schemes();
  244: 
  245: Returns an arrayref of the names of the categorical colour schemes this module
  246: can draw with, sorted alphabetically: C<category10>, C<paired>, C<set2>,
  247: C<set3> and C<tableau10>.
  248: These are the names the C<scheme> and C<color_scheme> options accept.
  249: 
  250: =cut
  251: 
  252: sub categorical_schemes
  253: {
  254: 	return [ sort keys %CATEGORICAL_SCHEMES ];
  255: }
  256: 
  257: =head2 palette
  258: 
  259:     my $colours = HTML::D3->palette('set2');
  260: 
  261: Returns an arrayref of the colours of one scheme - the same colours, in the same
  262: order, that a chart drawn with C<< scheme => 'set2' >> draws with.
  263: A map, a legend or a static renderer can therefore colour the same data the way
  264: the chart does, from Perl, without a browser:
  265: 
  266:     my $scheme  = 'set2';
  267:     my $colours = HTML::D3->palette($scheme);
  268:     my $index   = 0;
  269:     for my $category (@categories) {
  270:         printf "%s is %s\n", $category, HTML::D3->colour_at($scheme, $index++);
  271:     }
  272: 
  273: Dies with C<Unknown scheme: E<lt>nameE<gt>> for a name that is not in
  274: C<categorical_schemes()>, rather than quietly returning the default scheme's
  275: colours, so that a misspelt scheme is found where it is asked for.
  276: The arrayref returned is a copy: pushing onto it does not change the palette.
  277: 
  278: =head2 colour_at
  279: 
  280:     my $fill = HTML::D3->colour_at('tableau10', $index);
  281: 
  282: Returns the colour one scheme gives the C<$index>th category, counting from
  283: zero, cyclically: an index past the end of the palette wraps around to its
  284: start, which is how C<d3.scaleOrdinal> - what the snippets draw with - assigns
  285: colours.
  286: Colour 0 is the first colour of the scheme, the one C<render_histogram_snippet>
  287: uses as the bar fill when it is given a C<scheme>.
  288: 
  289: The British spelling matches the C<colour_by_series> option.
  290: Dies with C<Unknown scheme: E<lt>nameE<gt>> for an unknown scheme, and with
  291: C<Colour index must be a non-negative integer> for an index that is not one.
  292: 
  293: =cut
  294:

  460:

  464:

  832: =head2 render_pie_chart
  833: 
  834:     my $html = $chart->render_pie_chart($data);
  835:     my $html = $chart->render_pie_chart($data, { separator => ':' });
  836: 
  837: Generates HTML and JavaScript code to render a pie chart.
  838: Each slice is coloured with the ten hex values of C<d3.schemeCategory10>,
  839: inlined into the page rather than read from D3's own palette (see L</palette>);
  840: percentage labels appear inside each slice and a colour legend is shown to the
  841: right of the pie.
  842: Accepts the following arguments:
  843: 
  844: =over 4
  845: 
  846: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of data points.  Each data point is an
  847: array reference with two elements: the label (string) and the value (numeric).
  848: 
  849: =item * C<%opts> - Optional hashref of options.
  850: 
  851: =over 8
  852: 
  853: =item * C<separator> (string, default C<'/'>) - Character shown between the
  854: label and value in the SVG legend.
  855: 
  856: =back
  857: 
  858: =back
  859: 
  860: Returns a string containing the complete HTML5 document.
  861: 
  862: =head3 Errors
  863: 
  864: =over 4
  865: 
  866: =item * Throws C<Data is not optional> when C<$data> is C<undef>.
  867: 
  868: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of arrays> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
  869: 
  870: =back
  871: 
  872: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
  873: 
  874: =head4 Input
  875: 
  876:     {
  877:         data => {
  878: 		type => 'arrayref',
  879: 		element_type => [ 'string', 'number' ]
  880: 	},
  881:         opts => { type => 'hashref', optional => 1, default => {} },
  882:     }
  883: 
  884:     Each element of C<$data> is C<[ Str, Num ]>; passing C<undef> or a
  885:     non-arrayref dies.
  886:     Recognised C<opts> key: C<separator> (string, default C<'/'>)
  887:     - character shown between label and value in the SVG legend.
  888: 
  889: =head4 Output
  890: 
  891:     Str -- complete HTML5 document; pie rendered with C<d3.pie()> and
  892:            C<d3.arc()>; slices coloured with category10's hex values,
  893:            inlined; percentage label inside each slice; legend to the right.
  894: 
  895: =cut

 1175: =head2 render_pie_chart_snippet
 1176: 
 1177:     my $fragment = $chart->render_pie_chart_snippet(\@slices);
 1178:     my $fragment = $chart->render_pie_chart_snippet(\@slices, \%opts);
 1179:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - always 'pie_chart'
 1180:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable fragment; caller must load D3 v7
 1181: 
 1182: Generates an embeddable pie or donut chart fragment for use in existing HTML
 1183: layouts.  Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'pie_chart', html =E<gt> Str }>.  The
 1184: caller is responsible for loading D3 v7 before embedding the fragment.
 1185: 
 1186: =head3 Data format
 1187: 
 1188: Each element of C<\@slices> is C<[$label, $value]> or C<[$label, $value, \%extra]>.
 1189: Negative values are silently converted to their absolute value.  Zero-value
 1190: slices are silently omitted.  C<\%extra> key/value pairs are shown as
 1191: additional rows in the hover tooltip.
 1192: 
 1193: =head3 Options (C<\%opts>)
 1194: 
 1195: =over 4
 1196: 
 1197: =item * C<animated> (bool, default 0) - fan slices in from arc-length 0 on
 1198: first render using C<attrTween> / C<d3.easeBackOut> (800 ms, staggered).
 1199: Respects C<prefers-reduced-motion>.
 1200: 
 1201: =item * C<donut> (bool, default 0) - render as a donut chart (inner radius
 1202: 38% of outer radius); the total sum appears in the centre hole.
 1203: 
 1204: =item * C<sort_slices> (string, default C<'none'>) - C<'value'> for
 1205: largest-first, C<'label'> for alphabetical, C<'none'> for input order.
 1206: 
 1207: =item * C<max_slices> (int, default 0) - when E<gt> 0, only the top N-1
 1208: slices are shown individually; the rest are collapsed into an C<"Other"> slice.
 1209: 
 1210: =item * C<legend> (bool, default 1) - render an HTML legend panel beside the chart.
 1211: 
 1212: =item * C<color_scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical
 1213: colour scheme.  Supported: C<tableau10>, C<category10>, C<set2>, C<set3>,
 1214: C<paired> - the names C<categorical_schemes()> returns, whose colours C<palette()>
 1215: makes available to Perl.
 1216: 
 1217: =item * C<separator> (string, default C<'/'>) - Character shown between the
 1218: label and value in each legend entry (e.g. C<'/'> produces
 1219: C<Label / 12.34 (42.0%)>, C<':'> produces C<Label : 12.34 (42.0%)>).
 1220: 
 1221: =back
 1222: 
 1223: =head3 Errors
 1224: 
 1225: =over 4
 1226: 
 1227: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of arrays> when C<\@slices> is not an ARRAY reference.
 1228: 
 1229: =back
 1230: 
 1231: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 1232: 
 1233: =head4 Input
 1234: 
 1235:     {
 1236:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 1237:         opts => { type => 'hashref', optional => 1, default => {} },
 1238:     }
 1239: 
 1240:     Each element of C<$data> is C<[ Str, Num ]> or C<[ Str, Num, HashRef ]>;
 1241:     passing C<undef> or a non-arrayref dies.
 1242:     Recognised C<opts> keys: C<animated> (boolean, default C<0>),
 1243:     C<donut> (boolean, default C<0>), C<sort_slices> (string: C<'value'>,
 1244:     C<'label'>, or C<'none'>; default C<'none'>), C<max_slices> (integer,
 1245:     default C<0>), C<legend> (boolean, default C<1>),
 1246:     C<color_scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>),
 1247:     C<separator> (string, default C<'/'> - shown between label and value in
 1248:     legend entries).
 1249: 
 1250: =head4 Output
 1251: 
 1252:     HashRef -- C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'pie_chart', html =E<gt> Str }>;
 1253:                embeddable fragment; no DOCTYPE, no page shell, no D3 CDN tag.
 1254: 
 1255: =cut

 1503: =head2 render_heatmap_snippet
 1504: 
 1505:     my $fragment = $chart->render_heatmap_snippet(\@triples);
 1506:     my $fragment = $chart->render_heatmap_snippet(\@triples, \%opts);
 1507:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - always 'heatmap'
 1508:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable fragment; caller must load D3 v7
 1509: 
 1510: Generates an embeddable grid heatmap for use in existing HTML layouts.
 1511: Each cell sits at the intersection of an X-axis label and a Y-axis label;
 1512: its colour encodes the cell's numeric value using a sequential D3 colour
 1513: scale.  Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'heatmap', html =E<gt> Str }>.  The
 1514: caller is responsible for loading D3 v7 before embedding the fragment.
 1515: 
 1516: =head3 Data format
 1517: 
 1518: Each element of C<\@triples> is C<[$x_label, $y_label, $value]>.
 1519: C<$value> must be numeric or C<undef> (C<undef> rows are silently
 1520: skipped).  Zero is a valid value and maps to the lightest cell colour.
 1521: The caller is responsible for any aggregation: if multiple triples share
 1522: the same (x_label, y_label) pair, the last one wins.
 1523: 
 1524: =head3 Options (C<\%opts>)
 1525: 
 1526: =over 4
 1527: 
 1528: =item * C<color_scheme> (string, default C<'YlOrRd'>) - D3 sequential
 1529: colour scheme.  Supported: C<YlOrRd>, C<Blues>, C<Greens>, C<Purples>,
 1530: C<RdPu>, C<YlGnBu>.
 1531: 
 1532: =item * C<x_label> (string, default C<''>) - Axis title below the X axis.
 1533: The value is JavaScript-escaped (backslash, double-quote, newline, carriage
 1534: return, C<E<lt>>) before being embedded in the page; other characters are taken
 1535: literally.
 1536: 
 1537: =item * C<y_label> (string, default C<''>) - Axis title left of the Y axis.
 1538: Same JS-escaping as C<x_label> applies.
 1539: 
 1540: =item * C<val_label> (string, default C<'Value'>) - Tooltip value label.
 1541: Same JS-escaping as C<x_label> applies.
 1542: 
 1543: =item * C<show_values> (bool, default 0) - Print value inside each cell.

 1841: 
 1842: =head2 render_bar_chart_snippet
 1843: 
 1844:     my $result = $chart->render_bar_chart_snippet(\@bars);
 1845:     my $result = $chart->render_bar_chart_snippet(\@bars, \%opts);
 1846: 
 1847: Generates an embeddable D3.js v7 bar chart fragment.  Returns a hashref
 1848: C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'bar_chart', html =E<gt> $str }> where C<$str> is a
 1849: self-contained HTML fragment (no page shell, no D3 CDN tag) that the caller
 1850: embeds directly after loading D3.js.  C<$str> is a Perl character string
 1851: with the UTF-8 flag set (or pure ASCII when all labels are ASCII).
 1852: 
 1853: Each element of C<\@bars> is an array reference:
 1854: 
 1855:     [ $label, $value ]
 1856:     [ $label, $value, \%extra ]
 1857: 
 1858: C<$label> is the category name (string); C<$value> is a non-negative number
 1859: (negative values are silently converted to their absolute value); the optional
 1860: C<\%extra> hashref supplies additional key/value pairs shown in the hover
 1861: tooltip.  Data points with an undefined C<$value> are silently skipped.
 1862: 
 1863: =head3 Options (C<\%opts>)
 1864: 
 1865: =over 4
 1866: 
 1867: =item C<orientation> (string, default C<'vertical'>)
 1868: 
 1869: C<'vertical'> draws bars rising from a horizontal category axis.
 1870: C<'horizontal'> draws bars extending from a vertical category axis.
 1871: 
 1872: =item C<sort_bars> (string, default C<'none'>)
 1873: 
 1874: Sort order applied before rendering: C<'value'> sorts descending by bar
 1875: height, C<'label'> sorts ascending alphabetically, C<'none'> preserves the
 1876: input order.
 1877: 
 1878: =item C<max_bars> (integer, default C<0>)
 1879: 
 1880: When non-zero, only the first C<max_bars> entries (after any sort) are
 1881: rendered; the remaining entries are collapsed into a single C<Other> bar
 1882: whose value equals their sum.  Zero means no limit.
 1883: 
 1884: =item C<color> (string, default C<'steelblue'>)
 1885: 
 1886: Either a CSS colour value (e.g. C<'steelblue'>, C<'#4e79a7'>) applied to all
 1887: bars, or the special token C<'categorical'>, which colours each bar
 1888: distinctively using D3's Tableau-10 palette.
 1889: 
 1890: A value holding anything a CSS colour cannot, such as a quote or C<E<lt>>, is
 1891: ignored and the default used.
 1892: 
 1893: =item C<show_values> (bool, default C<0>)
 1894: 
 1895: When true, the numeric value of each bar is printed above vertical bars or
 1896: to the right of horizontal bars.
 1897: 
 1898: =item C<animated> (bool, default C<0>)
 1899: 
 1900: When true, bars grow from the baseline on page load: vertical bars rise from
 1901: the bottom; horizontal bars extend from the left.  An 800 ms D3 transition
 1902: with a per-bar stagger up to 100 ms is used.  The animation is suppressed
 1903: when C<prefers-reduced-motion> is set in the viewer's OS.
 1904: 
 1905: =item C<value_label> (string, default C<'Value'>)
 1906: 
 1907: Label shown in the hover tooltip before the numeric value.  The value is
 1908: JavaScript-escaped before being embedded in the page.
 1909: 
 1910: =item C<x_label> (string, default C<''>)
 1911: 
 1912: When non-empty, a text label is rendered below the bottom axis.  The value
 1913: is JavaScript-escaped before being embedded in the page.
 1914: 
 1915: =back
 1916: 
 1917: =head3 Errors
 1918: 
 1919: =over 4
 1920: 
 1921: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of arrays> when C<$data> is not an
 1922: ARRAY reference.
 1923: 
 1924: =item * Throws C<Each data point must be an array reference> when an element
 1925: is not an array reference.
 1926: 
 1927: =item * Throws C<Each data point must have at least 2 elements> when an
 1928: element has fewer than 2 items.
 1929: 
 1930: =item * Throws C<Value must be numeric> when a C<$value> is not a number.
 1931: 
 1932: =item * Throws C<orientation must be 'vertical' or 'horizontal'> on an
 1933: invalid orientation value.
 1934: 
 1935: =item * Throws C<sort_bars must be 'value', 'label', or 'none'> on an
 1936: invalid sort_bars value.
 1937: 
 1938: =back
 1939: 
 1940: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 1941: 
 1942: =head4 Input
 1943: 
 1944:     {
 1945:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 1946:         opts => { type => 'hashref', optional => 1 },
 1947: 	orientation => { type => 'string', memberof => [ 'vertical', 'horizontal' ], optional => 1 },
 1948: 	sort_bars => { type => 'string', memberof => [ 'value', 'label', 'none' ], optional => 1 }
 1949:     }
 1950: 
 1951:     Each element of C<$data>: C<[ Str, Num ]> or C<[ Str, Num, HashRef ]>;
 1952:     undef C<$value> silently skipped; negative C<$value> becomes positive.
 1953: 
 1954: =head4 Output
 1955: 
 1956:     HashRef -- { svg_id => 'bar_chart', html => Str }
 1957:     html is a Perl character string (UTF-8 flag set, or pure ASCII).
 1958: 
 1959: =cut

 2296: 
 2297: =head2 render_line_chart_with_tooltips
 2298: 
 2299:     $html = $chart->render_line_chart_with_tooltips($data);
 2300: 
 2301: Generates HTML and JavaScript code to render a line chart with mouseover tooltips.
 2302: Accepts the following arguments:
 2303: 
 2304: =over 4
 2305: 
 2306: =item * C<$data> - An array reference containing data points. Each data point should
 2307: be an array reference with two elements: the label (string) and the value (numeric).
 2308: 
 2309: =back
 2310: 
 2311: Returns a string containing the HTML and JavaScript code for the chart.
 2312: The JavaScript tooltip strings use C<< <\/b> >> (with a backslash) rather than
 2313: C<< </b> >> to satisfy html-tidy's requirement that C<< </ >> followed by a
 2314: letter not appear literally inside C<< <script> >> blocks.
 2315: 
 2316: =head3 Errors
 2317: 
 2318: =over 4
 2319: 
 2320: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of arrays> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
 2321: 
 2322: =back
 2323: 
 2324: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 2325: 
 2326: =head4 Input
 2327: 
 2328:     {
 2329:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 2330:     }
 2331: 
 2332:     Each element of C<$data> is C<[ Str, Num ]>; passing C<undef> or a
 2333:     non-arrayref dies.
 2334: 
 2335: =head4 Output
 2336: 
 2337:     Str -- complete HTML5 document; mouseover tooltip reveals label and value.
 2338:            Tooltip strings use C<< <\/b> >> not C<< </b> >>.
 2339: 
 2340: =cut
 2341: 
 2342: sub render_line_chart_with_tooltips
 2343: {
 2344: 	my $self = shift;
 2345: 	my $raw  = Params::Get::get_params('data', \@_) // {};
 2346: 	die 'Data must be an array of arrays' unless ref($raw->{data}) eq 'ARRAY';
 2347: 	Params::Validate::Strict::validate_strict({

 2367:     <style>
 2368: 	.tooltip {
 2369: 	    position: absolute;
 2370: 	    background-color: white;
 2371: 	    border: 1px solid #ccc;
 2372: 	    padding: 5px;

 2384:     <script@{[ $self->_nonce_attr ]}>
 2385: 	const data = $json_data;

 2390: 	const margin = { top: 20, right: 30, bottom: 40, left: 40 };
 2391: 	const width = $self->{width} - margin.left - margin.right;
 2392: 	const height = $self->{height} - margin.top - margin.bottom;

 2457: 
 2458: =head2 render_line_chart_snippet
 2459: 
 2460:     my $fragment = $chart->render_line_chart_snippet($data);
 2461:     my $fragment = $chart->render_line_chart_snippet($data, \%opts);
 2462:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - the id attribute of the <svg> element
 2463:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable HTML fragment (style + svg + script)
 2464: 
 2465: Generates an embeddable HTML fragment for a line chart with mouseover tooltips.
 2466: Unlike C<render_line_chart_with_tooltips>, this method returns a fragment with
 2467: no C<<!DOCTYPE>>, C<<html>>, C<<head>>, or C<<body>> wrapper, suitable for
 2468: splicing directly into a Mojolicious TT (or any other) layout.
 2469: 
 2470: The caller is responsible for loading D3 in the page C<<head>>, e.g.:
 2471: 
 2472:     <script@{[ $self->_nonce_attr ]} src="https://d3js.org/d3.v7.min.js"></script>
 2473: 
 2474: =head3 Arguments
 2475: 
 2476: =over 4
 2477: 
 2478: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of data points. Each point is an array
 2479: reference with two required elements - the label (string) and the value
 2480: (numeric) - and an optional third element: a hash reference of extra key/value
 2481: pairs to display in the tooltip after the label and value rows.
 2482: 
 2483:     [$x, $y]          # basic point
 2484:     [$x, $y, \%row]   # point with extra tooltip data
 2485: 
 2486: =item * C<\%opts> - Optional hash reference of rendering options:
 2487: 
 2488: =over 4
 2489: 
 2490: =item * C<id> (string, default C<'chart'>) - Override the C<id> attribute of
 2491: the C<<svg>> element.  Use this when embedding multiple charts on the same page.
 2492: 
 2493: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, default C<0>) - If true, emit
 2494: C<viewBox="0 0 W H" width="100%">, with no C<height>, instead of fixed pixel
 2495: dimensions, so the chart scales with its container.  Falls back to
 2496: C<< $self->{responsive} >> when not set per call.
 2497: 
 2498: =back
 2499: 
 2500: =back
 2501: 
 2502: =head3 Return value
 2503: 
 2504: A hash reference with:
 2505: 
 2506: =over 4
 2507: 
 2508: =item * C<svg_id> - The C<id> attribute used on the C<<svg>> element.
 2509: 
 2510: =item * C<html> - The embeddable fragment string (Perl character string).
 2511: 
 2512: =back
 2513: 
 2514: =head3 Errors
 2515: 
 2516: =over 4
 2517: 
 2518: =item * Dies with C<'Data must be an array of arrays'> when C<$data> is not an
 2519: ARRAY reference.
 2520: 
 2521: =back
 2522: 
 2523: =head3 Side effects
 2524: 
 2525: None.  The method is read-only.
 2526:

 2528: 
 2529:     {
 2530:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 2531:         opts => {
 2532:             type     => 'hashref',
 2533:             optional => 1,
 2534:             keys     => {
 2535:                 id         => { type => 'string',  optional => 1 },
 2536:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 2537:             },
 2538:         },
 2539:     }
 2540:

 2544: {

 2594:     const width = $w - margin.left - margin.right;
 2595:     const height = $h - margin.top - margin.bottom;
 2596:

 2598: 	.domain(data.map(d => d.label))
 2599: 	.range([0, width]);
 2600:

 2602: 	.domain([0, d3.max(data, d => d.value)])
 2603: 	.nice()
 2604: 	.range([height, 0]);
 2605:

 2607: 	.attr("transform", `translate(\${margin.left},\${margin.top})`);

 2611: 	.y(d => y(d.value));

 2661: 
 2662: =head2 render_zoomable_line_chart_snippet
 2663: 
 2664:     my $fragment = $chart->render_zoomable_line_chart_snippet($data);
 2665:     my $fragment = $chart->render_zoomable_line_chart_snippet($data, { animated => 1 });
 2666:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - the id attribute of the <svg> element
 2667:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable HTML fragment (style + button + svg + script)
 2668: 
 2669: Like C<render_line_chart_snippet>, but adds brush-to-zoom: the user can drag
 2670: across a range of the x-axis to zoom into that region. A I<Reset zoom> button
 2671: (hidden until a zoom is active) returns the chart to its original extent.
 2672: Subsequent brushes on the zoomed view zoom in further; Reset always returns to
 2673: the full dataset.
 2674: 
 2675: The caller is responsible for loading D3 in the page C<<head>>.
 2676: 
 2677: Accepts the same arguments as C<render_line_chart_snippet>: an array reference
 2678: of data points, each C<[$x, $y]> or C<[$x, $y, \%extra]>, plus an optional
 2679: second argument C<$opts> (hashref).
 2680: 
 2681: =head3 Options
 2682: 
 2683: =over 4
 2684: 
 2685: =item * C<animated> (boolean, default C<0>) - when true, the initial page load
 2686: animates the line drawing left-to-right via the C<stroke-dashoffset> technique
 2687: (1200 ms, C<d3.easeLinear>), then fades in data-point circles after the line
 2688: finishes (300 ms after a 1200 ms delay).  Respects
 2689: C<prefers-reduced-motion>: when the user has requested reduced motion the line
 2690: is drawn immediately at full opacity.  Subsequent zoom and reset redraws are
 2691: never animated regardless of this flag.
 2692: 
 2693: =back
 2694: 
 2695: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 2696: 
 2697: =head4 Input
 2698: 
 2699:     {
 2700:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 2701:         opts => { type => 'hashref', optional => 1, default => {} },
 2702:     }
 2703: 
 2704:     Each element of C<$data> is C<[ Str, Num ]> or C<[ Str, Num, HashRef ]>;
 2705:     passing C<undef> or a non-arrayref dies.
 2706:     Recognised C<opts> key: C<animated> (boolean, default C<0>).
 2707: 
 2708: =head4 Output
 2709: 
 2710:     HashRef -- C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'chart', html =E<gt> Str }>;
 2711:                embeddable fragment; no DOCTYPE, no page shell, no D3 CDN tag.
 2712: 
 2713: =head3 Errors
 2714: 
 2715: Dies with I<Data must be an array of arrays> if C<$data> is not an arrayref.
 2716: 
 2717: =cut

 2956: =head2 render_multi_series_line_chart_with_tooltips
 2957: 
 2958:     $html = $chart->render_multi_series_line_chart_with_tooltips($data);
 2959: 
 2960: Generates HTML and JavaScript code to render a chart of many lines with mouseover tooltips.
 2961: 
 2962: Accepts the following arguments:
 2963: 
 2964: =over 4
 2965: 
 2966: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of series hashes. Each element is a hashref
 2967: with a C<name> key (string) and a C<data> key (array reference of C<< {label, value} >>
 2968: hashrefs).
 2969: 
 2970:     [
 2971:         { name => 'Series A', data => [{ label => 'Jan', value => 100 }, ...] },
 2972:         ...
 2973:     ]
 2974: 
 2975: =back
 2976: 
 2977: Returns a string containing the HTML and JavaScript code for the chart.
 2978: Tooltip strings use C<< <\/b> >> rather than C<< </b> >> for html-tidy compliance.
 2979: 
 2980: =head3 Errors
 2981: 
 2982: =over 4
 2983: 
 2984: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of hashes> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
 2985: 
 2986: =back
 2987: 
 2988: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 2989: 
 2990: =head4 Input
 2991: 
 2992:     {
 2993:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 2994:     }
 2995: 
 2996:     Each element of C<$data> is a hashref with keys C<name> (string) and
 2997:     C<data> (arrayref of hashrefs with C<label> and C<value> keys);
 2998:     passing C<undef> or a non-arrayref dies.
 2999: 
 3000: =head4 Output
 3001: 
 3002:     Str -- complete HTML5 document; one coloured line per series with mouseover tooltips.
 3003: 
 3004: =cut
 3005: 
 3006: sub render_multi_series_line_chart_with_tooltips

 3038: 	}

 3724: =head2 render_multi_series_line_chart_snippet
 3725: 
 3726:     my $fragment = $chart->render_multi_series_line_chart_snippet(\@series);
 3727:     my $fragment = $chart->render_multi_series_line_chart_snippet(\@series, \%opts);
 3728:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - the id attribute of the <svg> element
 3729:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable HTML fragment (style + svg + script)
 3730: 
 3731: Generates an embeddable multi-series line chart fragment.  Unlike
 3732: C<render_multi_series_line_chart_with_interactive_legends>, this method returns
 3733: a bare fragment with no C<< <html> >>, C<< <head> >>, or C<< <body> >> wrapper;
 3734: D3.js must be loaded by the host page.
 3735: 
 3736: =head3 Arguments
 3737: 
 3738: =over 4
 3739: 
 3740: =item * C<\@series> - An array reference of series.  Each series is a hash
 3741: reference with a C<name> (string) and a C<data> array reference of
 3742: C<< { label => Str, value => Num } >> points:
 3743: 
 3744:     [
 3745:         { name => 'North', data => [ { label => 'Jan', value => 12 }, ... ] },
 3746:         { name => 'South', data => [ { label => 'Jan', value =>  8 }, ... ] },
 3747:     ]
 3748: 
 3749: Series need not share labels: the X domain is the union of every series' labels,
 3750: in the order C<x_order> gives.  One line and one set of dots is drawn per series, and a
 3751: clickable legend toggles each series on and off.
 3752: 
 3753: =item * C<\%opts> - Optional hash reference:
 3754: 
 3755: =over 4
 3756: 
 3757: =item * C<scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical colour
 3758: scheme (for example C<'category10'>, C<'set2'>, C<'set3'>, C<'paired'>).
 3759: C<categorical_schemes()> lists the names, and C<palette()> hands a Perl caller
 3760: the colours of one.
 3761: 
 3762: =item * C<x_label> (string, default C<''>) - Label shown below the X axis.
 3763: 
 3764: =item * C<y_label> (string, default C<''>) - Label shown beside the Y axis.
 3765: 
 3766: =item * C<animated> (boolean, default 0) - If true each line draws itself left
 3767: to right by animating its stroke dash offset.  Respects
 3768: C<prefers-reduced-motion>.
 3769: 
 3770: =item * C<x_order> (C<'auto'>, C<'data'> or C<'sort'>, default C<'auto'>) - the
 3771: order of the X domain.  C<'data'> takes the labels in the order they are met,
 3772: series by series, as before 0.29.  C<'sort'> sorts them: numerically when every
 3773: label is a number, otherwise as strings.  C<'auto'> sorts them when every label
 3774: is a number or an ISO 8601 date or date-time (C<2026-01-15>, C<2026-01>,
 3775: C<2026-01-15T09:30>), and otherwise keeps the order they are met in, so that
 3776: C<Jan>, C<Feb>, C<Mar> stay in the caller's order.  Taken as met, two series of
 3777: dates gave an axis such as C<15, 19, 16, 17>.  Whenever the labels are sorted,
 3778: each line's points are drawn in the axis order.  The caller's series are not
 3779: changed.
 3780: 
 3781: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 3782: 
 3783: =item * C<id> (string, default C<'chart'>) - C<id> of the C<<svg>> element.
 3784: 
 3785: =back
 3786: 
 3787: =back
 3788: 
 3789: Returns C<< { svg_id => 'chart', html => Str } >>.
 3790: 
 3791: =head3 Errors
 3792: 
 3793: =over 4
 3794: 
 3795: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of series> when C<\@series> is not an
 3796: ARRAY reference.
 3797: 
 3798: =item * Throws C<Each series must be a hashref with name and data> when a
 3799: series is not a hashref, has no C<name>, or has a C<data> that is not an ARRAY
 3800: reference.
 3801: 
 3802: =item * Throws C<Unknown x_order: E<lt>valueE<gt>> when C<x_order> is not
 3803: C<auto>, C<data> or C<sort>.
 3804: 
 3805: =back
 3806: 
 3807: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 3808: 
 3809: =head4 Input
 3810: 
 3811:     {
 3812:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 3813:         opts => {
 3814:             type     => 'hashref',
 3815:             optional => 1,
 3816:             keys     => {
 3817:                 scheme     => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'tableau10' },
 3818:                 x_label    => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 3819:                 y_label    => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 3820:                 x_order    => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'auto' },
 3821:                 animated   => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 0 },
 3822:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 3823:                 id         => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'chart' },
 3824:             },
 3825:         },
 3826:     }
 3827: 
 3828: =head4 Output
 3829: 
 3830:     HashRef -- { svg_id => 'chart', html => Str }
 3831: 
 3832: =cut

 4065: tooltips.  Returns a fragment with no C<<!DOCTYPE>>, C<<html>>, C<<head>>, or

 4069: 
 4070: =over 4
 4071: 
 4072: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of data points.  Each point is an array
 4073: reference with two required numeric elements (x, y), an optional third hash

 4075: fourth hash reference carrying the C<series> (colour group) and C<size> (bubble
 4076: radius) dimensions:
 4077: 
 4078:     [$x, $y]          # basic point
 4079:     [$x, $y, \%row]   # point with extra tooltip data
 4080:     [$x, $y, \%row, { series => 'North', size => 42 }]   # colour and size
 4081: 
 4082: =item * C<\%opts> - Optional hash reference:
 4083: 
 4084: =over 4
 4085: 
 4086: =item * C<id> (string, default C<'scatter_chart'>) - C<id> of the C<<svg>> element.
 4087: 
 4088: =item * C<color> (CSS colour or C<'categorical'>, default C<'steelblue'>) -
 4089: Fill colour for the data points.  C<'categorical'> uses the Tableau-10 palette.
 4090: A value holding anything a CSS colour cannot, such as a quote or C<E<lt>>, is
 4091: ignored and the default used.
 4092: 
 4093: =item * C<x_label> (string, default C<''>) - Label for the X axis.
 4094: 
 4095: =item * C<y_label> (string, default C<''>) - Label for the Y axis.
 4096: 
 4097: =item * C<value_label> (string, default C<'Value'>) - Prefix for the tooltip value row.
 4098: 
 4099: =item * C<colour_by_series> (boolean, default 0) - Colour each point by its
 4100: C<series> value instead of by point index, and draw a legend listing the series
 4101: to the right of the plot.  Ignored for points with no C<series> value.
 4102: 
 4103: =item * C<scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical colour
 4104: scheme used when C<colour_by_series> is set, for example C<'category10'>,
 4105: C<'set2'>, C<'set3'>, or C<'paired'>.
 4106: C<categorical_schemes()> lists the names, and C<palette()> hands a Perl caller
 4107: the colours of one.
 4108: 
 4109: =item * C<series_col> (string, default C<'Series'>) - Row label for the series
 4110: value in the tooltip.
 4111: 
 4112: =item * C<size_col> (string, default C<'Size'>) - Row label for the size value
 4113: in the tooltip.
 4114: 
 4115: =item * C<radius_range> (arrayref of two numbers, default C<[3, 15]>) - Pixel
 4116: radii mapped from the smallest and largest observed C<size> value.  The mapping
 4117: is by area (C<d3.scaleSqrt>), so a point twice as large is drawn with four
 4118: times the area.  A point with no C<size> value is drawn at the lower radius.
 4119: 
 4120: =item * C<animated> (boolean, default 0) - If true, circles fade in from
 4121: opacity 0 on page load (400 ms).  Respects C<prefers-reduced-motion>.
 4122: 
 4123: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, default 0) - See C<render_bar_chart_snippet>.
 4124: 
 4125: =back
 4126: 
 4127: =back
 4128: 
 4129: =head3 Return value
 4130: 
 4131: A hash reference with C<svg_id> (string) and C<html> (Perl character string).
 4132: 
 4133: =head3 Errors
 4134: 
 4135: =over 4
 4136: 
 4137: =item * Dies with C<'Data must be an array of arrays'> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY ref.
 4138: 
 4139: =item * Dies with C<'Each data point must be an array reference'> when an element is not an ARRAY ref.
 4140: 
 4141: =item * Dies with C<'Each data point must have at least 2 elements'> when a point has fewer than 2 items.
 4142: 
 4143: =item * Dies with C<'X value must be numeric'> when the x element is not a number.
 4144: 
 4145: =item * Dies with C<'Y value must be numeric'> when the y element is not a number.
 4146: 
 4147: =back

 4445: =head2 render_table_snippet
 4446: 
 4447:     my $fragment = $chart->render_table_snippet($data);
 4448:     my $fragment = $chart->render_table_snippet($data, \%opts);
 4449:     # $fragment->{table_id} - the id attribute of the <table> element
 4450:     # $fragment->{html}     - embeddable HTML fragment (style + table + script)
 4451: 
 4452: Generates an embeddable HTML fragment for a sortable, filterable data table.
 4453: Returns a fragment with no page-shell wrapper; the caller loads D3 if needed.
 4454: 
 4455: =head3 Arguments
 4456: 
 4457: =over 4
 4458: 
 4459: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of row array references.  The first row
 4460: is treated as the header row.  Every subsequent row must have the same number
 4461: of columns as the header.
 4462: 
 4463:     [ ['Name', 'Value', 'Category'],   # header
 4464:       ['Alpha',   300,  'A'],
 4465:       ['Beta',    150,  'B'],
 4466:     ]
 4467: 
 4468: =item * C<\%opts> - Optional hash reference:
 4469: 
 4470: =over 4
 4471: 
 4472: =item * C<id> (string, default C<'data_table'>) - C<id> of the C<<table>> element.
 4473: 
 4474: =item * C<sortable> (boolean, default 1) - If true, clicking a column header
 4475: sorts the table by that column (toggle ascending/descending).
 4476: 
 4477: =item * C<caption> (string, default C<''>) - Optional C<<caption>> element text.
 4478: 
 4479: =back
 4480: 
 4481: =back
 4482: 
 4483: =head3 Return value
 4484: 
 4485: A hash reference with C<table_id> (string) and C<html> (Perl character string).
 4486: Note: returns C<table_id>, not C<svg_id>, because this method renders an HTML
 4487: table rather than an SVG chart.
 4488: 
 4489: =head3 Errors
 4490: 
 4491: =over 4
 4492: 
 4493: =item * Dies with C<'Data must be an array of arrays'> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY ref.
 4494: 
 4495: =item * Dies with C<'Data must have at least one row (header row)'> when C<$data> is empty.
 4496: 
 4497: =item * Dies with C<'Each row must be an array reference'> when a row is not an ARRAY ref.
 4498: 
 4499: =back
 4500: 
 4501: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 4502: 
 4503:     {
 4504:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 4505:         opts => {
 4506:             type     => 'hashref',
 4507:             optional => 1,
 4508:             keys     => {
 4509:                 id       => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'data_table' },
 4510:                 sortable => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 1 },
 4511:                 caption  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 4512:             },
 4513:         },
 4514:     }
 4515: 
 4516: =cut

 4640: =head2 render_stacked_bar_snippet
 4641: 
 4642:     my $result = $chart->render_stacked_bar_snippet(\@rows, \%opts);
 4643: 
 4644: Generates an embeddable stacked bar chart (no page shell, no CDN tag).
 4645: 
 4646: Each element of C<\@rows> is a hashref with three keys named by C<opts>:
 4647: C<cat_col> (the X-axis grouping), C<seg_col> (the stacking segment), and
 4648: C<val_col> (the numeric measure).  Multiple rows sharing the same
 4649: cat+seg combination are summed.
 4650: 
 4651: Accepted C<\%opts> keys:
 4652: 
 4653: =over 4
 4654: 
 4655: =item * C<cat_col> (string, default C<'cat'>) - row key for the X-axis category.
 4656: 
 4657: =item * C<seg_col> (string, default C<'seg'>) - row key for the stacking segment.
 4658: 
 4659: =item * C<val_col> (string, default C<'val'>) - row key for the numeric value.
 4660: 
 4661: =item * C<orient> (string C<'v'> or C<'h'>, default C<'v'>) - bar orientation.
 4662: 
 4663: =item * C<scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical colour scheme.
 4664: Supported: C<tableau10>, C<category10>, C<set2>, C<set3>, C<paired> - the names
 4665: C<categorical_schemes()> returns, whose colours C<palette()> makes available to
 4666: Perl.
 4667: 
 4668: =item * C<x_label> (string, optional) - axis label shown below the category axis.
 4669: 
 4670: =item * C<sort> (string, optional) - sort categories: C<'value'> (desc total)
 4671: or C<'label'> (alpha).
 4672: 
 4673: =item * C<id> (string, optional) - override the default C<svg_id> value.
 4674: 
 4675: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 4676: 
 4677: =back
 4678: 
 4679: Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'stacked_bar', html =E<gt> Str }>.
 4680: 
 4681: =head3 Errors
 4682: 
 4683: =over 4
 4684: 
 4685: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of hashes> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
 4686: 
 4687: =item * Throws C<Each data point must be a hash reference> when a row is not a hashref.
 4688: 
 4689: =item * Throws C<Value must be numeric> when a value field is not a number.
 4690: 
 4691: =item * Throws C<orient must be 'v' or 'h'> for an invalid C<orient> value.
 4692: 
 4693: =item * Throws C<sort must be 'value' or 'label'> for an invalid C<sort> value.
 4694: 
 4695: =back
 4696: 
 4697: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 4698: 
 4699: =head4 Input
 4700: 
 4701:     {
 4702:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 4703:         opts => {
 4704:             type     => 'hashref',
 4705:             optional => 1,
 4706:             keys     => {
 4707:                 cat_col  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'cat' },
 4708:                 seg_col  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'seg' },
 4709:                 val_col  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'val' },
 4710:                 orient   => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'v' },
 4711:                 scheme   => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'tableau10' },
 4712:                 x_label  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 4713:                 sort     => { type => 'string',  optional => 1 },
 4714:                 id       => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'stacked_bar' },
 4715:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 4716:             },
 4717:         },
 4718:     }
 4719: 
 4720: =head4 Output
 4721: 
 4722:     HashRef -- { svg_id => Str, html => Str }
 4723: 
 4724: =cut

 4935: =head2 render_combo_snippet
 4936: 
 4937:     my $result = $chart->render_combo_snippet(\@rows, \%opts);
 4938: 
 4939: Generates an embeddable combo chart: bars for one measure on the left Y
 4940: axis, a line for another measure on the right Y axis, sharing the same
 4941: X (category) axis.
 4942: 
 4943: Each element of C<\@rows> is a hashref with at least three keys named by
 4944: C<opts>: C<x_col> (X-axis label), C<bar_col> (bar measure), and
 4945: C<line_col> (line measure).
 4946: 
 4947: Accepted C<\%opts> keys:
 4948: 
 4949: =over 4
 4950: 
 4951: =item * C<x_col> (string, default C<'x'>) - row key for the X-axis label.
 4952: 
 4953: =item * C<bar_col> (string, default C<'bar_val'>) - row key for the bar measure.
 4954: 
 4955: =item * C<line_col> (string, default C<'line_val'>) - row key for the line measure.
 4956: 
 4957: =item * C<bar_label> (string, default C<'Bar'>) - legend/axis label for bars.
 4958: 
 4959: =item * C<line_label> (string, default C<'Line'>) - legend/axis label for line.
 4960: 
 4961: =item * C<bar_color> (string, optional CSS colour, default C<'steelblue'>) - bar fill.
 4962: A value holding anything a CSS colour cannot, such as a quote or C<E<lt>>, is
 4963: ignored and the default used.
 4964: 
 4965: =item * C<line_color> (string, optional CSS colour, default C<'#e15759'>) - line stroke.
 4966: Checked as C<bar_color> is.
 4967: 
 4968: =item * C<scheme> (string, optional) - a categorical scheme, as
 4969: C<categorical_schemes()> lists them.  The bars take its first colour and the
 4970: line its second, as C<colour_at($scheme, 0)> and C<colour_at($scheme, 1)>.
 4971: C<bar_color> and C<line_color> still win when given.  Any other name is
 4972: ignored and the defaults used.
 4973: 
 4974: =item * C<id> (string, optional) - override the default C<svg_id> value.
 4975: 
 4976: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 4977: 
 4978: =back
 4979: 
 4980: Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'combo_chart', html =E<gt> Str }>.
 4981: 
 4982: =head3 Errors
 4983: 
 4984: =over 4
 4985: 
 4986: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of hashes> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
 4987: 
 4988: =item * Throws C<Each data point must be a hash reference> when a row is not a hashref.
 4989: 
 4990: =item * Throws C<Bar value must be numeric> when a bar value field is not a number.
 4991: 
 4992: =item * Throws C<Line value must be numeric> when a line value field is not a number.
 4993: 
 4994: =back
 4995: 
 4996: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 4997: 
 4998: =head4 Input
 4999: 
 5000:     {
 5001:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 5002:         opts => {
 5003:             type     => 'hashref',
 5004:             optional => 1,
 5005:             keys     => {
 5006:                 x_col      => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'x' },
 5007:                 bar_col    => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'bar_val' },
 5008:                 line_col   => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'line_val' },

 5204: 
 5205: =head2 render_treemap_snippet
 5206: 
 5207:     my $result = $chart->render_treemap_snippet(\@rows_or_tree, \%opts);
 5208: 
 5209: Generates an embeddable treemap (no page shell, no CDN tag).
 5210: Leaf area is proportional to the numeric measure.
 5211: 
 5212: Accepts two data formats:
 5213: 
 5214: =over 4
 5215: 
 5216: =item * B<Flat path> (arrayref of hashrefs): each row must have a C<path>
 5217: key (e.g. C<'Root/A/X'>) and a C<value> key.  The module converts this
 5218: to a D3 stratify hierarchy in JS.  Override the path separator with
 5219: C<opts-E<gt>{path_sep}>.
 5220: 
 5221: =item * B<Nested tree> (hashref): a pre-built D3-compatible hierarchy
 5222: with C<name>/C<children>/C<value> structure.  Passed directly to
 5223: C<d3.hierarchy()>.
 5224: 
 5225: =back
 5226: 
 5227: B<Colour by value.>  A flat row, or a node of the tree, may also carry
 5228: C<color_value>, a second number that colours its tile: the area says how much
 5229: and the colour says how well -- sales sized by revenue and coloured by margin.
 5230: As soon as one tile has one, every tile is shaded from the sequential
 5231: C<color_scheme> over the smallest to the largest C<color_value>, in place of
 5232: its top-level group's colour; a tile without one is grey.  The tooltip gives
 5233: the value under C<color_label>, a legend at the foot of the chart gives the
 5234: range, and labels on light tiles are drawn dark.  When every tile has the same
 5235: value they all take the middle of the scheme.
 5236: 
 5237:     ->render_treemap_snippet([
 5238:         { path => 'All/North', value => 1200, color_value => 0.18 },
 5239:         { path => 'All/South', value =>  800, color_value => 0.05 },
 5240:     ], { color_scheme => 'Blues', color_label => 'Margin' });
 5241: 
 5242: Accepted C<\%opts> keys:
 5243: 
 5244: =over 4
 5245: 
 5246: =item * C<path_sep> (string, default C<'/'>)  - path separator (flat format only).
 5247: 
 5248: =item * C<scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical colour scheme.
 5249: Supported: C<tableau10>, C<category10>, C<set2>, C<set3>, C<paired> - the names
 5250: C<categorical_schemes()> returns, whose colours C<palette()> makes available to
 5251: Perl.
 5252: 
 5253: =item * C<label> (boolean, default C<1>) - show node labels inside cells.
 5254: 
 5255: =item * C<color_scheme> (string, default C<'YlOrRd'>) - the sequential scheme
 5256: tiles are coloured from when they carry C<color_value>: C<YlOrRd>, C<Blues>,
 5257: C<Greens>, C<Purples>, C<RdPu> or C<YlGnBu>, as for C<render_heatmap_snippet>.
 5258: Not read when no tile has a C<color_value>.
 5259: 
 5260: =item * C<color_label> (string, default C<'Colour'>) - what C<color_value>
 5261: measures, for the tooltip and the legend.
 5262: 
 5263: =item * C<id> (string, optional) - override the default C<svg_id> value.
 5264: 
 5265: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 5266: 
 5267: =back
 5268: 
 5269: Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'treemap', html =E<gt> Str }>.
 5270: 
 5271: =head3 Errors
 5272: 
 5273: =over 4
 5274: 
 5275: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of hashes or a hash tree>
 5276: when C<$data> is neither an ARRAY nor a HASH reference.
 5277: 
 5278: =item * Throws C<Each flat row must be a hash reference> when a flat row is not a hashref.
 5279: 
 5280: =item * Throws C<Each flat row must have a 'path' key> when a flat row lacks C<path>.
 5281: 
 5282: =item * Throws C<Each flat row must have a numeric 'value' key> when a value is not numeric.
 5283: 
 5284: =item * Throws C<Each flat row's 'color_value' must be numeric> when a flat
 5285: row's C<color_value> is defined but not a finite number.
 5286: 
 5287: =item * Throws C<Each node's 'color_value' must be numeric> when a node of the
 5288: tree has a C<color_value> that is defined but not a finite number.
 5289: 
 5290: =item * Throws C<Unknown color_scheme: E<lt>nameE<gt>> when a tile has a
 5291: C<color_value> and C<color_scheme> is not one of the sequential schemes.
 5292: 
 5293: =back
 5294: 
 5295: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 5296: 
 5297: =head4 Input
 5298: 
 5299:     {
 5300:         data => { type => 'arrayref | hashref' },
 5301:         opts => {
 5302:             type     => 'hashref',
 5303:             optional => 1,
 5304:             keys     => {
 5305:                 path_sep => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '/' },
 5306:                 scheme   => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'tableau10' },
 5307:                 label    => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 1 },
 5308:                 color_scheme => { type => 'string', optional => 1, default => 'YlOrRd' },
 5309:                 color_label  => { type => 'string', optional => 1, default => 'Colour' },
 5310:                 id       => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'treemap' },
 5311:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 5312:             },
 5313:         },
 5314:     }
 5315: 
 5316: =head4 Output
 5317: 
 5318:     HashRef -- { svg_id => Str, html => Str }
 5319: 
 5320: =cut

 5563: 
 5564: =head2 render_histogram_snippet
 5565: 
 5566:     my $result = $chart->render_histogram_snippet(\@values, \%opts);
 5567: 
 5568: Generates an embeddable frequency histogram (no page shell, no CDN tag).
 5569: C<\@values> is an arrayref of numeric scalars (already extracted and
 5570: coerced by the caller).
 5571: 
 5572: Accepted C<\%opts> keys:
 5573: 
 5574: =over 4
 5575: 
 5576: =item * C<bins> (integer or C<undef>, default C<20>) - number of bins.
 5577: Pass C<undef> for automatic bin count using Sturges' rule:
 5578: C<ceil(log2(n) + 1)>.  Ignored when C<binned> is set.
 5579: 
 5580: =item * C<binned> (boolean, default 0) - treat C<$data> as pre-computed bins
 5581: rather than as raw values.  Each element is a hashref with a C<count> and
 5582: either a C<label> or a numeric C<lo>/C<hi> pair from which the label C<[lo,hi)>
 5583: is derived:
 5584: 
 5585:     { label => '[0,10)', count => 3 }
 5586:     { lo => 10, hi => 20, count => 5 }
 5587: 
 5588: The caller owns the binning, so C<d3.bin()> is never called: one bar is drawn
 5589: per entry, in the order supplied, and the supplied counts are authoritative.
 5590: 
 5591: The bin labels are drawn rotated.  The plot gives up as much room as they need,
 5592: up to half the chart's height and a quarter of its width, so that they and
 5593: C<x_label> do not overlap and stay inside the chart.
 5594: 
 5595: =item * C<scheme> (string, optional) - D3 categorical colour scheme (for example
 5596: C<'tableau10'> or C<'category10'>) whose first colour is used as the bar fill
 5597: when C<color> is not given.
 5598: That colour is C<colour_at($scheme, 0)>, so a caller shading the same data by
 5599: hand starts from the same colour the bars do.
 5600: 
 5601: =item * C<x_label> (string, optional) - label shown below the X axis.
 5602: 
 5603: =item * C<color> (string, default C<'steelblue'>) - bar fill colour.
 5604: A value holding anything a CSS colour cannot, such as a quote or C<E<lt>>, is
 5605: ignored and the default used.
 5606: 
 5607: =item * C<id> (string, optional) - override the default C<svg_id> value.
 5608: 
 5609: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 5610: 
 5611: =back
 5612: 
 5613: Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'histogram', html =E<gt> Str }>.
 5614: 
 5615: =head3 Errors
 5616: 
 5617: =over 4
 5618: 
 5619: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of numeric values> when C<$data> is
 5620: not an ARRAY reference.
 5621: 
 5622: =item * Throws C<Each value must be numeric> when any element is non-numeric.
 5623: 
 5624: =item * Throws C<bins must be a positive integer> when C<bins> is defined
 5625: but not a positive integer.
 5626: 
 5627: =item * Throws C<Data must contain at least one bin> when C<binned> is set and
 5628: C<$data> holds no bins.
 5629: 
 5630: =item * Throws C<Each pre-binned entry must be a hash reference> when a
 5631: pre-binned element is not a hashref.
 5632: 
 5633: =item * Throws C<Bin count must be numeric> when a pre-binned C<count> is
 5634: missing or not numeric.
 5635: 
 5636: =item * Throws C<Bin count must not be negative> when a pre-binned C<count> is
 5637: negative.
 5638: 
 5639: =item * Throws C<Each pre-binned entry must have a label or numeric lo and hi>
 5640: when a pre-binned entry has neither a C<label> nor a numeric C<lo>/C<hi> pair.
 5641: 
 5642: =back
 5643: 
 5644: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 5645: 
 5646: =head4 Input
 5647: 
 5648:     {
 5649:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 5650:         opts => {
 5651:             type     => 'hashref',
 5652:             optional => 1,
 5653:             keys     => {
 5654:                 bins    => { type => 'integer', optional => 1, default => 20 },
 5655:                 binned  => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 0 },
 5656:                 x_label => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 5657:                 color   => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'steelblue' },
 5658:                 scheme  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1 },
 5659:                 id      => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'histogram' },
 5660:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 5661:             },
 5662:         },
 5663:     }
 5664: 
 5665: =head4 Output
 5666: 
 5667:     HashRef -- { svg_id => Str, html => Str }
 5668: 
 5669: =cut
 5670:
